  • Her heading: her face, her stage in the Uma loop as a chip ("Acquaintance", "Friend", "Farmhand", "Sweetheart", "Engaged", "Married"), her level in words in her stage's colour, her ● Ready chip; under them her favourite line, her birthday as a farm day ("★ Spring 4") and her house once she has one. A spouse waiting for a room says "Needs a room", and an own-house wisher sharing or waiting says softly "She'd love her own house".
  • The route as one track: F1–F10, ◆, R1–R10, ◇, ◇, S1–S5 (28 steps), the played ones filled in their ladder's colour, the next filling by her bar, the rest empty, a waiting rung drawn as a diamond. It wraps to two rows of 14 on a narrow phone.
  • The Next card: "Next: Romance 5"; its perk in her line's words (with "Opens when the Mine is built" where her line's place waits); her beat's hint for the rung (where and how it opens); the bar with "points / cost" and "+N banked"; and what it waits for: "Ready", "123 to go", "Tomorrow", "Needs a ring", "Tomorrow: one event a day", "The Fields, when she works there", "With Carrot in the Warehouse", "Bond 3 with Stand-in 2", "Opens when the Mine is built", "Opens when the Kitchen is built" (her chapter's gate), "She said “Not yet”", "Comes tonight", and once her route is done "Her route is complete". It never waits on the story: an event the pack doesn't script plays the game's card.
    • Play · 1 action › (48 pt, in her ladder's colour) plays her Ready event, an action (a special day's event actions first).
    • A setup not met says what to do: "Put Carrot in the Warehouse, then come back.", "Let her and Stand-in 2 work side by side until bond 3."
    • After a Not yet, Ask her ›, once a day ("You asked her today. Ask again tomorrow.").
    • While the proposal needs a ring and the ring bench stands, Make one at the Forge's bench ›.
  • Chapters: her chapters, each with its number (✓ once every event has played), its title and its events played of all ("3/12"). The one she's in is in her ladder's colour, with its events as chips, played ✓ and to come; a chapter whose gate doesn't hold yet says what it needs ("Opens when the Kitchen is built"). Under them, what opens the next: "Next · Kitchen days · After Friendship 6 · Opens when the Kitchen is built". Once her route is complete: "Her last chapter is closed. Life at home goes on."
  • Perks: the reached ones by ladder ("F1–F10 ✓ · R1–R4 ✓"), what each reached one does now, the next, and All 25 perks on a tap (reached ✓ with "Now · …", the next in bold, the rest greyed).
  • From her: her pet name for you from Romance 1 ("Calls you “Sprout”"; without her bible's, your own name), and her keepsake from Friendship 10 with its effect and her line about it (without her bible's, "Her keepsake" and a plain line).
  • Gifts: her loved, liked and disliked goods as learnt ("?" until then), each group's points at ★1, and "Others +10"; with no tastes written, "Any gift +10". From Friendship 7, her closest friend's loved gift ("Stand-in 1 loves Golden Carrot").
  • Ties: each tie with the other Uma's face, its kind (Friends, Rivals, Mentor, Teammates, Sisters, Close) and their bond hearts, closest first (the strongest tie, then the most bond points, then the ties' order), her closest friend marked Closest; the points to the next heart ("Friends · 150/300"); Read while a conversation of theirs is opened and unread; "Not here yet" for an Uma not on the farm.
  • Memories: her count ("15 of 26") and Album ›.
  • Gallery: for an Uma with illustrations, the ones seen of all hers ("3 of 9") and Open › (her gallery).
  • Ring: from Romance 8, "Her ring will read: ‘…’" (the engraving, her Romance 8 beat's caption); from the proposal, her ring with its bonus ("Iron Ring · +3% at the Fields once you're married", then "+3% at the Fields"), "Her ring reads: ‘…’", and Give her the ‹ring› › for each better ring in the Warehouse.
  • The actions, 48 pt in the sheet's foot: Chat · 1 (an action, +15; then her line for the day; "Chat ✓" once done), Gift · 1 (an action: the picker; "Gift ✓"), both greyed with no actions left or when she isn't in front of you, ☾ Evening · 2 › while she can spend one and the day has its two actions (otherwise greyed with why: "Evening ✓" spent with her, "Evening · F3" below Friendship 3, "In town" while she's out there and you aren't, "At home" on a day she's off the map, "Evening spent" with someone else, "Evening · 2" short of the actions), Invite her to work · 1 on the row under them while she's a friend not yet working the farm, and Praise +20 while she's earned it. What an act pays floats up from her face, and a note says it ("She likes it. +36").
  • Where you meet her: Chat, Gift, her evening and Invite her to work need her in front of you (where you meet her): she lives on the farm or works it by day, or you've gone into town today and she's out there. Otherwise a line above them says where she is, "In town today, at the Café. Talks and gifts are in person." with Find her › (her menu where she is, /town?at=<place>&uma=<id>, where going into town comes first), or "At home today. Find her in town on her days.". Play · 1 action › (her Ready event) works from anywhere.
  • Where to find her (one who lives in town), under her face, read from her fixed schedule (who's where): Today, her place ("The Café", with Find her ›), or "At home · next out Spring 4"; then this season's days and places in short words, one line a place in the order of its first day: "Spring · The Café · 1, 4, 7, 10", her job's "The Bakery, at work · 1–6", and home "At home · the other days" (her story's home on the map by its name). A farmhand reads "Today · On the farm" and "Every day · On the farm by day, after her job: Helps out at the Café".
  • Hearts | Work: the Work side is her work sheet: her favourite line, the day she joined, where she is, "Farmhand since Spring 3 · her tasks" (a tap to the work board) once she works the farm, her fourteen aptitudes (each level, a bar to the next and the trick it opened; "where she'd start" before she's a farmhand: see aptitude), and her nine activities with talent, rank, points and the next challenge.

Her chapters

Each Uma's story is hers, in her own chapters, from the day you meet her to married life. There's no farm-wide story. Her chapters frame her route; the backbone stays as built (her arrival, friendship F1–F10 and the confession, romance R1–R10, the proposal and the wedding, married life S1–S5), and so do the points, the perks and the evenings along it.

 her cast entry's `chapters` ──► her chapters along her route ──► the one she's in: the chapter holding her next rung
   (else the four defaults)       in the order of their first events      │
                                                                          ▼
 a chapter's gate (optional) ──► holds back its first event ──► Ready says "gate": "Opens when the Kitchen is built"
                                    │ the gate holds and the rung before has played
                                    ▼
                        the chapter opens ──► the morning: "A new chapter with ‹name› · Kitchen days"
                                    │ its events play as Ready allows
                                    ▼
                        Spouse 5 ──► her last chapter closes; the household goes on after it
  • Her chapters are her cast entry's chapters: how many, their titles, which of her rungs each holds and an optional gate (the story pack's menu). With none, the four defaults:
#ChapterIts events
1Getting to know herHer meeting scene, F1–F10, her confession
2TogetherR1–R10, the proposal
3The weddingThe wedding
4Married lifeS1, S3, S5
  • Along her route: a chapter's events are its rungs in the route's order, and her chapters run in the order of their first events. A rung the pack leaves out counts in the chapter before it along the route (else the first), a rung in two counts in the first, and a chapter holding no rung the game plays is left out. A chapter without a title shows as "Chapter 2". S2 and S4, which come at night, count with S1 and S3.
  • The one she's in is the chapter holding her next rung. A chapter opens when its first event can play: the rung before it has played and its gate holds.
  • A chapter's gate asks the farm for something, from the trigger menu: a place built, a Farm Rank, a good shipped, another's event, a season or a festival (the town's kinds, talks, a gift and a spot, are meeting her own: a gate of one is an error, and skipped). Until it holds, the chapter's first event waits, whatever her bar: Ready's gate says it ahead of a short bar. Once that event has played the chapter is open, and its other events follow as Ready allows, even when a season's or a festival's day has passed. A gate on the chapter holding her meeting scene never holds the scene back (her meet says when she's met): it holds her first event.
Gate (gate)HoldsHer profile says
builtThis place of the farm is built: it stands the night after it’s paid for“Opens when the Kitchen is built”
farm-rankThe Farm Rank reaches this rank, at night“Opens at Farm Rank 4”
shippedThis many of a good shipped, in all: the bin pays at night“Opens when you’ve shipped 50 Carrot”
eventAnother character’s event has played (<characterId>:<event>; arrival is her meeting scene), or an event id: anyone’s“Opens after Stand-in 2’s Friendship 3”
seasonThis season: from its first morning to its last“Opens in Winter”
festivalThis festival’s day, any year“Opens on the Harvest Cup”
  • Where you see them:
    • her profile's Chapters: every chapter, the one she's in with its events played and to come, a gate's words, and what opens the next (her profile);
    • the People page: her chapter at a glance on her row, "Ch. 2 · Together" (the People page);
    • the morning: "A new chapter with ‹name› · Together", tagged New, the morning one opens: the morning after the event before it played (one event a day, so its first can't play sooner), or the morning its gate begins to hold. Her first chapter opens with meeting her.
  • Her ending: her last chapter closes when Spouse 5 plays, in married life. The household's scenes, the evenings and the house go on after it.
  • The save holds nothing new for them: her chapter is worked out from her route and the gates as it shows. The morning's line keeps her id and the chapter's number; its title is read from the pack.

Perks

A perk comes with every level, 25 in all, and every perk acts. A perk is read from her level, never stored: she has a rung's perk once its event has played (S2 and S4 once they've come at night). Only her signature dish writes anything to the save.

The perks that ride on an Uma's own work (the romance term on a harvest or a haul, side by side, her line's pay, the mentor, her keepsake on her work, bonds at work) act on a farmhand's (farmhands); an Uma who doesn't work the farm gets none of them. The night chores, morning items, meals, her keepsake on her chores and the household's act as written.

The perks' words

The words are templates by her favourite line. A perk that works at her line's place says "Opens when the Kitchen is built" (her place) while that place isn't built, and does nothing until then. The first table words each perk for a forager; the second shows what the place-bound ones say for every line.

At her line

Each place-bound template means one thing at each line's place. Every line whose place stands acts: farming, foraging and fishing from day 1, mining once the Mine (or a quarrier's Quarry) stands, the ranch once the Coop or the Barn does. The workshops' lines wait for their games, and a morning item of theirs is a crop of the season meanwhile. Her line is her favourite (her bible's, else her calling's, else master data's), as for every line; her +10% acts where her work is that line's place (the board's task she's set, at night), and her chores and morning items wherever she lives on the farm.

Her line+10% at her line (F2, R5)A night chore (F6, R7, S4)A morning item (R2, S2)Acts
FarmingEach plot she tends: +10% chance of an extra crop at harvest, at the plot’s starsWaters one dry growing tile (step 2)A ★2+ crop of the seasonYes
ForagingHer haul ×1.1, and her Sawmill batch ×1.1One item from her rank’s deepest area this season, at a day’s haul’s stars (step 4)A ★2+ good of her rank’s deepest forest area this seasonYes
FishingHer catch ×1.1, and her Smokehouse batch ×1.1One fish from her deepest spot this season, at a day’s haul’s stars (step 4)A ★2+ fish of her deepest spot this seasonYes
Mining (the Mine)Her haul ×1.1, and her Smelter batch ×1.1One ore from the Mine’s deepest floor reached, at a face’s stars (step 4)A ★2+ ore of the Mine’s deepest floor reachedYes, once the Mine stands
Mining (the Quarry)Her haul ×1.1, and her Cutter batch ×1.1One stone or gem from the Quarry’s deepest floor reached, at a face’s stars (step 4)A ★2+ stone or gem of the Quarry’s deepest floor reachedYes, once the Quarry stands
RanchThe goods she collects at the Coop or the Barn ×1.1: a +10% chance of an extra each, at its stars+5 fondness to each animal in her building: the Coop or the Barn she cares for on the work board, else the first that stands (step 3)A ★2+ egg, milk or fleece of an animal the farm keeps (a crop of the season with none)Yes, once the Coop or the Barn stands

Forging+10% goods as the Forge’s assistant+5% refund on the Forge’s next toolA ★2+ Fitting (a crop of the season meanwhile)Not yet: once the Forge’s game is built
  • Seeded: a fraction becomes a whole item by a seeded draw on the account, the day and her id, so a night replays the same.
  • Gathering and stations pay at night: the items an Uma's work finished tonight come to × (bond × (1 + romance)) in all. Side by side's pot pays first (×1.1, or ×1.2 in her element), then the perks' pot: the bond term × the romance term of the same items (her own bonuses only at her line's place, the crew's anywhere), as seeded copies (the same goods and stars) straight into the Warehouse. A forager's Sawmill batch, a fisher's Smokehouse batch, a miner's Smelter batch and a quarrier's Cutter batch count as her line. The job's own plan never changes.
  • Mining pays on a farmhand's faces the same way: the finds of her faces at the Mine (a quarrier's at the Quarry) are her night's items there, so her haul comes to ×1.1 at F2 and ×1.2 at R5, held to the caps.
  • The ranch pays on what she collects: the goods her care task collects at the Coop or the Barn are her night's items there, so each has her romance term's chance (+10% at F2) of an extra at its stars. They show with every line's in Hearts' "Perks · +1 item", not in the building's line. Her keepsake lifts the stars of what she collects there too.
  • Farming pays on a farmhand's harvests the same way: her harvested crops are her night's items at the Fields, so the perks' extras copy them by her romance term at night. The farmer's own harvests get none.

The romance term and its caps

Every romance bonus on one job is one capped number:

WhatCapHow
Her own line bonuses: F2 +10%, R5 +10%, her ring +1–15% once married+35%At her line’s place only (romanceTerms)
The crew’s: a dream’s +5% (the best at a place, never summed), the household’s +5% at 5 and +5% at 15 married+15%Anyone working the place (the household anywhere)
All romance bonuses on any one job, added+40%The romance term (romanceBonus)
In her element (×1.2)Outside the capThe bond term (extrasShare)
Night chores3 a night eachF6, R7, S4
Morning items1 a day each by her count, +1 on a morning in her houseEvery 4th from R2, every 2nd from S2
MentorOne a placeThe most hearts, then the cast’s order
Rank points from her ladder×1.10F5 ×1.05, F10 ×1.10 (loveRankFactor)

Worked: a married forager with F3, R5 and a grade-8 ring has +28% of her own (28% after her cap); a dream at the forest (+5%) and 15 married (+10%) add +15%; together +43%, held to +40%. Beside a 3-heart partner in her element her night’s items come to ×1.2 × 1.4 = ×1.68.

  • Her own (Friendship 3's +10%, Romance 5's +10% and her ring once married) counts only at her line's place, and only once that place is open. It's held to +35%.
  • The crew's (the best dream at this place, never summed; the household's +5% from 5 married and +5% more from 15) counts for anyone working there, the household anywhere. It's held to +15%.
  • Together they're held to +40%.
  • In her element (Friendship 8) sits outside the cap, in the bond term: side by side ×1.2 in place of ×1.1 for her at her line's place beside an Uma she has 3+ bond hearts with, on her night's extras and on a farmer's tended growth.

Night chores

  • 1 from Friendship 6, 2 from Romance 7, 3 from Spouse 4, never more, for an Uma on the farm (home in the Dorm, or a spouse in her house) whose line's place is open. One in town does none. Each chore is its own seeded pick.
  • Farming (at night, as crops grow): each chore waters one dry growing tile, a seeded pick, so it grows a stage tonight; with none dry, the chore does nothing. The morning says "Night chores · 2 tiles watered".
  • The ranch (at night, after the animals' night, step 3): each chore gives every animal in her building +5 fondness (held to 200). Her building is the first Coop or Barn she cares for on the work board, else the first of them that stands; one with no animals gets nothing.
  • Foraging and fishing (at night, after the hauls): one item from her rank's deepest open forest area this season, or one fish from her deepest open spot, by the season's shares, at the stars a day's haul there would have (her rank, talent, tool, well fed and keepsake), straight into the Warehouse.
  • Mining (at night, after the hauls): one find from the deepest floor reached at the Mine (an ore) or, for a quarrier, the Quarry (a stone or a gem), by a face's grade shares, at the stars a face there would have (her rank and talent in mining, the Pick or the Hammer, well fed and keepsake), straight into the Warehouse. The dig's count and its stairs don't move.
  • The morning says them in one line: "Night chores · 3 items", "Night chores · 2 items · +0.5 growth", "Night chores · +10 fondness".

Morning items

  • From Romance 2 she leaves a ★2+ good of her line every 4th morning, counted from the day she reached Romance 2. From Spouse 2 it's every 2nd morning at ★3+, counted from the day she reached Spouse 2.
  • One a day by her count, on the farm only. A morning in her house brings one more (see the house's morning, below).
  • The good is her line's (as her anniversary item, with its own draw): a miner's a find of the dig's deepest floor reached, a rancher's the good of an animal the farm keeps (its kinds alike). Its stars are a seeded draw of a day's work at her line, keepsake included (a miner's a face's, a rancher's her building's animals' hearts on average), held at the minimum (★2, or ★3 from Spouse 2).
  • The morning says "♥ Stand-in 2 left you ★3 Shiitake", opening her profile.

The mentor

At night, at each place farmhands worked today, an Uma at Friendship 9 whose line's place it is mentors the others who worked there: ×1.10 rank points for the day. One mentor a place: the most hearts (the furthest along her route), then the cast's order, then her id. She earns no bonus herself; a second Friendship 9 Uma there is mentored too.

Her keepsake

From Friendship 10 her keepsake adds +0.25 quality to her work at her line's place, +0.5 from Spouse 5 (her vow renewal), and nowhere else. It's read by her night chores and morning items, and by her work once the farmhands work. Its name and her line about it are her bible's (without one, "Her keepsake" and a plain line, “Keep it close. It’s for luck.”). It shows on her profile under From her, never in the Warehouse.

Her signature dish

Friendship 4's event writes her signature dish's recipe into the Kitchen's list, once (a recipe the farm already knows stays once), and her route keeps the dish so her profile can name it. The level-up card says "Recipe · ‹dish› · in the Kitchen's list". The dish is her loved good when that's a dish; otherwise her line's dish kind at the grade of her line's best place now:

Her lineHer dish kindIts gradeAt G1

ForagingSoupThe top grade of the deepest forest area anyone has worked (the Woods G2 … the World Tree G10)Button Mushroom Soup
FishingGrillThe top grade of the deepest spot anyone has worked, its fish at 10% or more (the Pond G2)Carp Grill
Mining (the Mine)CurryThe top grade of the Mine’s deepest floor reached (floors 1–3: G2 … floor 27 on: G10); G1 before it standsBasil Curry
Mining (the Quarry)CurryThe top grade of the Quarry’s deepest floor reached (floors 1–3: G2 … floor 27 on: G10); G1 before it standsBasil Curry
RanchCakeThe top grade of the goods the farm’s animals give (an Egg G1 … Cashmere Fleece G7); G1 with noneHoneycomb Cake
CookingTartG1 until the Kitchen’s game is builtApple Tart
ServingCookiesG1 until the Restaurant’s game is builtPeanut Cookies
ForgingBreadG1 until the Forge’s game is builtWheat Bread

Her loved good, when it’s a dish, is her signature dish as it is. The recipe joins workshops.recipes once (a recipe the farm knows stays once) and her route keeps its id (love.dish).

Her dream and breakfast

  • Her dream is her Spouse 3 event. Once it's in her Memories it acts: +5% for anyone at her line's place, in the crew's term. Two dreams at one place count once.
  • Breakfast is a spouse's Spouse 1 perk, served on a morning in her house: every Uma on the farm with no well-fed days left starts the day well fed for one day (a Rough meal, so that day's work speeds up and earns more rank points). A meal already running is never shortened or extended, and a breakfast isn't a staff meal (no staff-meal count moves). Each pair it feeds earns +5 bond.

On her profile

Her profile's Perks shows what each reached perk does now, a later perk taking the earlier one's line (Romance 5's replaces Friendship 3's): "R5 · +28% at the forest · +8% ring" (with "+5% crew" and "held to its cap" when they apply; a farmer's "+20% chance of an extra crop", a rancher's "of an extra good"), "R7 · 2 items each night" (a farmer's "+0.5 growth each night", a rancher's "+10 fondness each night"; "None while she lives in town"), "R2 · Next tomorrow" or "Next in 3 mornings", "F4 · ‹dish› · in the Kitchen's list", "F8 · Beside Stand-in 1" ("No 3-heart friend yet"), "F10 · Bent Trowel · +0.25 quality at the Fields", "R1 · She calls you “Sprout”", "R4 · With Stand-in 1", "R8 · “‹engraving›”". A perk whose place isn't built says "Opens when the Mine is built" (her place; the ranch's, "Opens when the Coop is built", opens with the Coop or the Barn) and nothing now.

Heart events and plays

Heart events

A level is its event. Once her next event is Ready it waits for you, for good: nothing fires on its own.

  • Four ways in: her Hearts row's ● Ready, her profile's Play · 1 ›, the morning's Ready line (its Play tag) and the calendar's Event ›. Each opens the event player over the page.
  • It costs one action, a special day's event actions first, spent as it's saved. With no action left of either kind, the player says "No actions left today." before it plays.
  • Her script or the game's card. Her route's event for the rung loads on demand; a rung the pack doesn't script plays the game's short card (her moment, her line, your reply), which pays as a written event does. A play with no prompt shows its game's ("Cast the line together.").
  • Nothing is saved while it plays. The player waits for the script and for master data. At the end one rule pays the event and moves her on, and the app commits it once, stamped. An event left halfway pays nothing and stays Ready.
  • One of hers a day: after one plays she isn't Ready again until tomorrow, however full her bar.

  • The place card fills the left, behind her bust (the dialogue engine's backdrop): a farm place's building frame on the season's ground (spring #dcefc7, summer #f6e7b0, autumn #f2d3b0, winter #e3ebf3), or a romance backdrop's borrowed frame and tint. A place step changes it mid-scene, and the title follows: "The Fields · Friendship 1", "The Pond at night · Romance 5". The speaker's bust is her portrait in the event's outfit (its own outfit, else its moment's: her wedding, her work, a picnic, the summer shore; else the day's season), her face the line's emote; during narration she keeps her last line's face. Without portraits, the blank bust (one of the town's people's too; a lettered disc for a role no one plays). A wedding's guests wear the day's season.

| Backdrop | Borrowed frame | Tint | |---|---|---| | The Pond at night | The Fishery's, under a night sky | #33456f | | The Overlook | The observatory's | #f0c493 | | The orchard bench | The orchard's | #f3dca6 | | The Dorm porch | The Dorm's | #ebc79f | | The harbour wall | The Warehouse's | #bcd6e2 | | The chapel bell | The chapel's | #f5e6bd | | The town | The exchange's | #f0d4b2 | | The hilltop track | The racecourse's | #d2e7b7 | | The sea pier | The fishery workbench's | #9fc6de |

  • The emote is her face: her portrait's, else the blank bust's (the dialogue engine).
  • Her bubble holds three lines at most, at 16 pt, typed out: a tap finishes the line, the next tap goes on, a hold skips. Narration and thoughts are italic. A pause goes on by itself (5 s at most).
  • Skip (or a hold on the bubble) jumps to the next choice or play, never past one. Auto goes on after a reading pause of 1.2 s plus 35 ms a character. Log lists the lines so far and your answers.
  • Which play: each play step in her script plays by the map below (a farming play may choose Pick, the default, or Rhythm with the hoe). With no play step anywhere in the event, her beat's "together" plays at the scene's end. Help and talk have no play. A play's good steps follow a Great or Perfect; its miss steps follow a Fine.

The plays

What the farmer and she do together is one short play on a flat sheet in the middle of the player. Each shows what to aim for first, takes one thumb (timing from the touch), takes nothing from the Warehouse, and closes on its grade. Never failed: a missed tap still lands, as a Fine. Every play is seeded by the account, the day, her id and the rung, so the same day plays the same. The five plays, at both lengths:

PlayYouGraded byIn an eventAs an evening
CastTap as a closing ring meets the mark; the line lands every timeTiming1 cast3 casts
PickTap her favourites (starred) among the goods popping up on a 3 × 3 fieldRight picks1 round of 103 rounds
RhythmTap on her beat with the activity’s mark (a hoe, a brush, a step, an oar…)Timing8 beats after her 43 × 8 beats
WalkWalk with her past today’s farm places; at each fork take a turnHer favourite turns found2 forks4 forks
ChoosePick one good of three for herHer taste1 pick—

A play scores 0–100 and grades by it:

GradeScoreAn event addsAn evening pays
Fine0–59+0+60
Great60–89+15+100
Perfect90–100+30+150

What plays as what. An activity whose game isn't built yet plays Rhythm with its mark (the fallback), with the same grades; when the real game ships, the event plays it. The marks are words until art draws them.

  • Walk's place: her line's first place when it's open today, else a seeded one of the open farm places her beats use, else the Fields. The path passes today's open farm places (not the Gate or the town). Before it she says "I love the Fields."
  • Pick's and Choose's goods: her loved and liked goods, starred; the others are the rest of their families' raw goods, grades 1–5, never one she dislikes. An Uma without a bible stars her line's first goods (and Choose finds every good neutral).

Choices and rewards

Each event has 1–2 choice points of 2–3 options (the confession, the proposal and the wedding up to 3), and every option shows its gain on the button before the tap: +30 for the best (the highest authored gain, each one tied at the top), +10 for the others. A romantic option carries a ♥. An option may also:

  • cost goods: "+10 · 1 Basil" on the button, taken at any stars, lowest first; greyed with "Need 1 Basil" when the Warehouse is short;
  • reveal a taste: "· learns a like" ("a love", "a dislike"), while that taste isn't known yet;
  • set a flag that changes later wording only: a later step plays one branch while the flag is set, the other otherwise.

No option scores below zero, removes a later event or changes the route. What an event pays, and its limits:

An eventPoints into her next bar
Played to its end+50
Each choice: the option with the highest authored gain (each one tied at the top)+30
Each choice: any other option+10
Each play: Fine, Great, Perfect+0, +15, +30
No play (help, talk): the best option taken a Perfect, any other a Great+30 or +15
A heart event’s usual size (any other is a note, and plays)1–2 choice points, at most 1 play
The confession’s, the proposal’s and the wedding’s usual size1–3 choice points, at most 3 plays

The sum goes into her next bar through the same rule as every act, so her anniversary's ×2 applies; then she moves up. The event also gives:

  • its rung's perk (Friendship 4's writes her signature dish's recipe here);
  • a Memory: the rung into her album, its caption her beat's line;
  • its unlock, if any: an item lands in the Warehouse at ★1; a place or a perk line is the card's to say;
  • any taste revealed or learnt by a Choose, and the path's flags;
  • her place in today's events (she's done for the day).

  • The sum: "+50 event", the choices ("+30 choice", "+40 choices"), each play's grade ("+15 Great") or, with no play, the best choice's grade; then "= +N toward ‹next rung›", "(×2 anniversary)" on her anniversary, and "(+N banked)". After a Not yet it reads "= +N, banked for Romance 1".
  • The rest: the perk, the Memory, any unlock, the recipe (Friendship 4), the ring (the proposal: "Iron Ring · hers now"), the wedding ("The Fields · the town’s bakery sends a cake · everyone well fed 1 day"), and the next rung with its points, its hint and its perk.
  • The stage-up screens follow three events, each with one Continue:
    • Partners (the confession's Yes): "Romance opens. Her bar turns rose.", the track re-coloured, and Opens now: the romance ladder, Romance 1's dates and her pet name, her partner chats, Romance 3's stargazing, Romance 6's dance and boat, Romance 9's footrace.
    • Engaged (the proposal): "A ring, and a yes. Your wedding comes next."; your wedding tomorrow, whenever you're ready; her ring and its bonus once you're married; a better ring any day, from her profile.
    • Married (the wedding): "Your wedding is done. Her bar turns gold."; the spouse ladder; her ring's bonus at her line; every evening kind her ladders opened; Spouse 1's breakfast; "Your anniversary: Summer 9, each year"; her room ("Her room: Farmhouse", or none free yet, so she keeps her Dorm bed) with buttons to move her to another house with a free room; "House mornings: +30 for her each morning you sleep there"; and "Married 1/20 · next household scene at 5" ("household scene 1 tomorrow" on a milestone's own wedding, "the Great Harvest tomorrow" on the 20th).
  • A night level (Spouse 2 or 4) plays from the morning's line (?level=<id>:<s2|s4>): first her level's scene, her route's s2 or s4, else the default story's, on the dialogue engine (Skip goes on); then the same card, with her last line of the scene, "Her bar filled overnight · now toward Spouse 3", its perk and her next rung. A level with no scene to play would show the card alone ("Her bar filled overnight: no event, the level comes by itself"); the default story writes both, so it doesn't happen.

The confession

Her confession is Ready the day after Friendship 10, at its own place: a longer scene with up to three plays and three choice points, its last choice point holding one Yes and one Not yet. Either way it pays like any event and counts as her event that day.

  • Yes moves her to romance at Romance 0, her banked points toward Romance 1, and shows Partners.
  • Not yet keeps her a close friend ("Close friend" in words). Nothing is lost: her points keep banking toward Romance 1, and her confession isn't Ready again.
  • Ask her › (her profile's Next card, while she's a close friend) plays the confession again from her line before its answer, without its plays, once a day, paying nothing, and not as her event: after a Yes there, her Romance 1 can play the same day if her bar is full and she hasn't played an event today. Yes clears the Not yet and shows Partners; Not yet: "Whenever you're ready. Ask again tomorrow."
  • She always confesses. An author may have her say "not yet" in an earlier scene's words, at no cost.
  • With her illustrations, her question (her lines into the answer, and the answer's choice) shows confession, her Yes shows confession-yes to the end, and a Not yet brings her bust back. Ask her shows them too.

Memories

Her album (from her profile's Album › and the Diary's Memories shelf) has 26 tiles, one a rung with an event, under four filters: Friend (Friendship 1–10 and her confession), Romance (Romance 1–10), Wed (the proposal and the wedding) and Spouse (Spouse 1, 3 and 5). Played tiles show their caption, the next is marked, the rest are locked. Her arrival › replays her arrival scene.

  • A replay starts from the top or from any choice point picked from its list ("Choice 1 · “Show me which ones.”"). It plays with its plays and her illustrations, every option is open (no cost, no gain shown), nothing is paid, taken or saved (but an illustration seen joins her gallery), and it ends "A Memory, replayed. Replays give no points."
  • The Diary's shelf lists each Uma on the farm with "n of 26", opening her album, and the household scenes and the finale once played.

The evening

The day has one evening across all the Umas, spent any time in the day from her profile (☾ Evening · 2 ›), her menu (Ask her out · 2, in town where she is or on the farm) or Tonight's row; an evening left empty costs nothing. It takes 2 of the day's actions (never a special day's event actions). It's a short play together at its evening length, graded Fine, Great or Perfect and never failed, that pays well, and more in her favourite kind; then her scene for it on the dialogue engine; then the card.

Who and the kinds

  • Who: a hangout with a friend, an Uma at Friendship 3 or more (her confession's wait and Romance 0 included), a date with a sweetheart from Romance 1 (the proposal's and the wedding's waits included), and every kind with a spouse ("An evening at home"). She must be in front of you (eveningHere): home on the farm (on the Dorm's list, or a spouse in her house), a farmhand on the farm by day, or out in town today with you there, where her menu asks her out. The kinds, their scenes and their points are the same wherever you ask her.
  • The kinds are the ten date games and two evenings of their own, opened by her ladder and by their game: Dinner in town (a date from Romance 3, in the dinner dress) and A night in (a date from Romance 6, in pajamas; at home once married), each played as a Pick. Cooking together and the footrace wait for their games, so they aren't offered yet (a favourite kind not offered still counts as hers, and she doesn't invite you to it). Festival night is a festival day's own slot.
  • Her scene: after the play, her route's scene for it, else the default story's: hangout-<kind> for a friend, date-<kind> for a sweetheart or a spouse, dinner-in-town and a-night-in for the two of their own; in its own outfit when it names one, else the moment's (a picnic's garden party dress, the dinner dress, pajamas, the kimono on festival night; else the day's season). Its choices are wording only, and Skip goes on to the card.
  • Her favourite kind is the date game her bible's beats use most; walk on a tie at the top, or with no date beats (or no bible). It pays ×1.5 and it's what her invitations ask for. Festival night is never her favourite.

Who: a hangout from Friendship 3 (her confession’s wait and Romance 0 included), a date from Romance 1 (the proposal’s and the wedding’s waits included), and every kind her ladders opened with a spouse (“An evening at home”); she must be in front of you: home on the farm, a farmhand there by day, or out in town today with you there. Each takes 2 of the day’s actions. The stand-in’s favourite kinds: 9000001 stargazing, 9000002 walk, 9000003 walk.

The points

The grade's 60, 100 or 150, ×1.5 in her favourite kind, ×1.5 on her birthday, ×1.5 on her anniversary: each once, multiplied, rounded half up, into her bar. An anniversary evening is ×1.5, not the day's ×2 as well. Her kinds sheet shows each kind's three numbers for her today before the tap ("Perfect +225"), and the play shows them under it. What each grade pays:

GradeAny kindHer favourite kind (×1.5)Her kind, on her birthdayHer kind, on her birthday and anniversary
Fine+60+90+135+203
Great+100+150+225+338
Perfect+150+225+338+506

An invitation said yes adds +20 with her first evening that day, whatever the kind; it’s an act of its own, so her anniversary doubles it (+40).

The slots and the invitations

Today's evening slots and this morning's invitations:

TodayEveningsFestival nightInvitations this morning
Any day1—1
From 10 married2 on days divisible by 4, else 1—2
A festival day (Spring 10, Summer 10, Autumn 10, Winter 10, Winter 12)As any day1 more, its own slot: only festival night fills itAs any day

An invitation’s draw: draw(uid, day, 'invite', n) among the Umas home at Friendship 3+ whose favourite kind is open to them, each weighted 1 + the days since her last evening (or since she arrived), up to 14; a second invitation is never the first’s Uma.

  • The slots: one evening a day; from 10 married, a second on days divisible by 4. Festival night is its own slot on a festival day (and on the Festival of Vows), filled only by its kind: a walk at the festival ground with anyone at Friendship 3 or more, 2 actions like any evening. An Uma may spend more than one slot in a day; the invitation's +20 comes with her first.
  • Invitations: each morning one Uma (two from 10 married, never the same one twice) asks you for her favourite kind tonight. Yes, tonight makes her Tonight's evening (refused once the evening is spent); Another day costs nothing, and she may ask again another morning. Each is answered once, and yesterday's clear at night.

└──────────────────────────────────────────┘
  • Tonight is End the day's sheet whenever a slot is free and someone can spend it (the day has the two actions), or anyone is married (then Tonight with, the night's companion, sits between the evening rows and the day's what's-left line). Otherwise the day ends as it does without the romance: at once when all is set, or after the one line. Whether Tonight opens is settled once, as the sheet opens, so coming back from a played evening shows its row ✓ and never ends the day by itself. End the day never waits on the evening.
  • The night is a fade: End the day dims the whole screen to dusk (#2b3557, 450 ms), the night runs under it with nothing shown, and the morning comes up from it (650 ms); with reduced motion, a plain cut; once married, it comes up on the night's companion's greeting (?life=morning:<id>), over the morning's card. A failed night lifts it at once and says Try again.
  • The morning's invitation: "✉ Stand-in 2: “Walk at dusk tonight?”" with Yes, tonight and Another day; said yes, "Tonight ✓" with Play › (her evening over the morning); declined, "Another day". Her row's ✉ stands while it's unanswered, or yes and not yet spent.

The ring, the wedding, and married life

The proposal needs a ring, so the old Forge opens early as a ring bench with no game. After the wedding comes the wedding night, then she moves in (the homestead), and married life goes on to the household's finale, with the household's meals and the night's companion (the Uma loop) along the way.

 an Uma's first Romance 8 event ──that night──► the Forge stands as the ring bench
   │                                              the morning: the smith's scene, "The Ring Bench" (once)
   ▼

   ▼

The ring bench

The bench's rule, in full:

The ring bench
OpensThe night after the first Romance 8 event anyone plays: buildings.forge = 0, and the smith’s scene the next morning
A ring of grade g3 refined goods of grade g, any family, any mix; the mining families’ (ingots, blocks, cut gems) once the Smelter and the Cutter stand
CostThe goods only: no gold, no game, no assistant, no score
OutThe ring, ★1 (a Rough make), into the Warehouse
How oftenOne ring a day (today.ring)
Her bonus once married+1% at her line a grade, inside her own +35%; the Forge’s merge game (not built yet) adds +1, +3 or +5 for a Fine, Great or Masterwork make
Refusals“The ring bench opens with your first Romance 8.” · “Three refined goods of one grade.” · “Ingots, blocks and cut gems open when the Smelter and the Cutter are built.” · “One ring a day.” · “Not enough in the Warehouse.”
  • On the map: until the bench stands the Forge's lock says "Opens with your first Romance 8". Then its quick look reads Ring bench · one ring a day with Make a ring › ("See the bench ›" once today's is made).
  • The smith's scene: the morning after, once the summary is put away (after the opening, before the meeting scenes), seven short lines and a card with See the bench or Later; read once.
  • The bench's sheet: the smith's face and name (the Uma who plays the role) and the rule; one row a grade you can make now, lowest first, each with the three goods it would take (the lowest-value refined goods of that grade, fewer stars first, any family, any mix), "→ Iron Ring" and "+3% once married". A tap on one of the three lists the grade's other goods held and swaps it in; a tap on a row chooses it. The Forge's own recipe shows greyed, "2 ingots + 1 cut gem · Opens when the Smelter and the Cutter are built"; then "Her bonus once married: +1% a grade", and Make an Iron Ring · 1 a day. Made, the ring is "in the Warehouse, ★1", and the foot says "One ring a day. Iron Ring made today ✓" until tomorrow. With nothing to make: "Nothing to make yet. Three refined goods of one grade make a ring: planks from the Sawmill, fillets from the Smokehouse, flour from the Mill."

Rings are goods of their own family: sold at the bin like any good (no season's demand), on the Warehouse's Rings tab while one is held, and a Diary row of their own (500g once all ten have shipped). One a day from goods worth a little less keeps the bench a ring maker, not a mint (an Iron Ring: 180g of planks becomes 340g).

The proposal

  • Ready the day after Romance 10, with any ring in the Warehouse ("Needs a ring" until then).
  • Play › first asks which ring, the best grade preselected; then her event plays (up to three plays and 1–3 choice points). It always succeeds: the ring leaves the Warehouse (lowest stars first), its grade becomes hers, she's engaged, and the stage-up reads Engaged.
  • Her ring: every ring is a Rough make, so her ring is its grade, 1–10, and once she's your spouse it's her bonus at her line, +1% a grade, inside her own +35%.
  • A better ring, any day from the proposal on: her profile offers each higher-grade ring in the Warehouse; she keeps it, and her old one comes back to the Warehouse at ★1. A lower or equal ring is refused.
  • The engraving: from Romance 8 her profile shows "Her ring will read: ‘…’", her Romance 8 beat's caption.

The wedding and the reception

  • Ready the day after the proposal, and it waits until you play it.
  • Play › asks the place first: her bible's wedding places (any number), else her wedding beat's place and the farm's defaults (the chapel bell, the Fields, the Overlook, the orchard bench), three different. Her event then plays on that place's card.
  • The guests are every spouse on the farm (the cast's order), then her two closest friends on the farm. Their faces line the top, they have no lines, and one line of narration comes first ("Stand-in 2 and Stand-in 3 came, in their best."; with none, "Just the two of you, and the whole farm quiet around you.").
  • The vows are its last choice point, paying as any choice. Your words and the place are kept as flags for later wording.
  • Then the reception: pick a dish from the Warehouse (the most well-fed days first); only with no dish held does the town's bakery send a cake. The dish is used. What it feeds:
The receptionWell fed from tomorrow, everyone on the farm
A ★1 dish1 day
A ★2 dish1 day
A ★3 dish2 days
A ★4 dish2 days
A ★5 dish3 days
No dish in the Warehouse: the town’s bakery sends a cake1 day (a Rough meal)

A meal already running is never shortened. The guests: every spouse on the farm, then her 2 closest friends on the farm (by bond); they have no lines. The places: her bible’s wedding.places, else her wedding beat’s place and chapel-bell, fields, overlook, orchard-bench, three different. Household milestones at 5, 10, 15, 20 married.

  • She becomes your spouse at Spouse 0, her wedding day noted (her anniversaries follow a year on), and takes her room at once (see Rooms and moves, below). That night everyone on the farm is well fed from tomorrow; two weddings in one day feed the farm by the longer reception.
  • The words of the bench, the proposal and the wedding say spouse, partner, vows, your wedding and you; the protagonist is a man, so a line may say the groom.
  • With her illustrations the wedding plays over its picture, wedding, from the guests' line to the vows' answer (the place card and the guests' faces give way to it).
  • The wedding night follows the wedding's card, before the Married stage-up: her route's wedding-night, else the default story's, in the wedding night outfit, tender and fading to morning. It pays nothing and saves nothing. With her illustrations it opens on wedding-night, and wedding-night-end comes up as it fades, after its last choice.
  • Moving in follows it, the morning after, before the Married stage-up: her route's moving-in, else the default story's ("A cart of her things rolls up the lane to the farm."), titled "Moving in · ‹her name›", as she moves to the farm: into the room she took at the wedding, in your home or a house built for her (rooms and moves below). One with no room free waits for one and moves in the morning a room is free; that morning's "⌂ ‹her name› moved into ‹house›" (Play) plays her moving in. It pays nothing and saves nothing; Skip ends it. Living on the farm, she's in the night's upkeep like any Uma there, spouses included (10g a night, upkeep); one who lived in town adds it as she moves in.

The homestead

Behind the yard the map grows the homestead: the Farmhouse, your own home with two rooms, from day 1, and six houses you build, name and extend.

  • House n opens to build with the n-th wedding, the one before it standing (they're built in turn). A house stands at once when bought, named there: 1–20 characters under the farmer's letters rule, a grey "Rose Cottage" placeholder, no two alike. An extension adds two rooms at once for half the house's price. The Farmhouse is never renamed or extended.
  • Never a gate: a wedding never waits for a room.
  • On the map: the homestead's rows sit after the yard and before the town edge and the Town, which stay the map's last rows. A row shows once it's open, its lots with it: the first (the Farmhouse, Houses 1 and 2) from day 1, the second (Houses 3–5) with three married, the third (House 6) with six; a house built shows whatever its row. A house's pips are its residents' faces (♥ on a Ready one) and its free rooms' dots; a tap opens the homestead's sheet at that house. The next lot shows the cottage dimmed and locked, "Build · 8,000g" once its wedding has come; a lot before its wedding says so ("Opens with your second wedding").

Rooms and moves

  • At her wedding she takes a room at once: her default is the house where her closest friend lives when it has room, else the first house with room, the Farmhouse first. An own-house wisher takes an empty house first, and everyone else's default passes over a house she lives in while another has room. The Married screen names her room and offers the other houses with a free room.
  • With no room free she keeps her Dorm bed, and her profile and Hearts row say "Needs a room". Each night, once a room is free (a house built or extended, or a room moved out of), every spouse home in the Dorm who waits takes one, in wedding order, by the same default, and the morning says so ("⌂ Stand-in 2 moved into Rose Cottage"). One who lives in town waits until she's home. You can also move her into a free room by hand at once.
  • Moves are at once and free, any time: on the homestead's sheet tap a face, then a free room in another house (a move) or another house's face (a swap). A spouse leaving or arriving by a Dorm swap waits for the morning.
  • A spouse in her house has no Dorm bed, so her bed is free for the next arrival, and she's on the farm: she takes jobs and tends, plays her events, spends evenings, does her chores, leaves her morning items, and eats breakfast and the reception with everyone.
  • Her own house: an own-house wisher is happiest alone in a house; while she shares or waits her profile says, softly, "She'd love her own house". Nothing is lost while she waits.

The night's house and its morning

  • Tonight's house is the night's companion's (Tonight, once anyone is married, so Tonight then opens every night): Tonight with picks a spouse with a room, and her house is tonight's (the night's companion). Each row shows her house, ☕ when a resident at Spouse 1+ would serve breakfast there tomorrow (someone's meal ends tonight and no reception is coming), and the nights since ("9 nights") for a house you haven't slept in for 8 nights or more. Without a pick the night takes the house slept in least lately (one never slept in first; ties the lower number; with no one housed, the Farmhouse) and its resident who's been your companion least lately. On a birthday-alone wisher's birthday her row is "Her room" ("her birthday, just you two"): picked, her morning is hers alone. A tap picks her at once; End the day never waits on it. The morning's "misses you" Tonight › picks a house (its resident who's been your companion least lately goes with it).
  • That night, before the bin, the house and the companion are noted (the house's night slept, her night) and the picks clear for tomorrow. In the morning she greets you. Later in the night each pair sharing a house earns +10 bond, and a spouse at Spouse 1 or 3 whose 600 is full reaches Spouse 2 or 4, with no Ready event (Spouse 2 starts her every-2nd-morning count); the morning's line plays its scene, then its card. Spouse 3 and 5 are always events.
  • The morning is that house's:
The morning of the house you slept in
Each resident+30 to her spouse bar (addLove, an anniversary’s ×2 applies)
A resident at S1+Her breakfast: everyone on the farm with no meal left well fed one day, once; +5 bond a pair it feeds
Each resident at S2+One more morning item of her line, ★3+ (lineGood, n = 2), in her own item line
A house lineOne resident’s line from the pack’s houseLines, else her temperament’s template
Her birthday aloneHer room: only her +30, breakfast and item
Not slept in for 8 nights“Rose Cottage misses you”, with Tonight › and Another night; never a penalty
Shared housesEvery night, +10 bond to each pair of residents (step 6)
The Festival of VowsThe finale’s day, each later year: every spouse +50, and a festival night
  • The house line is one resident's, drawn by the day: her own house lines from the pack, else her temperament's template with her name filled. Mornings only. The templates, original:
TemperamentHouse lines ({name} hers)
Breezy{name} is already out the door: “Race you to the porch!”<br>{name} hums at the window, toast in one hand. “Morning! The sky’s showing off.”<br>{name} swings in with the post. “Up, up. The day’s waiting for us.”
Steady{name} sets two cups down without a word and slides yours closer.<br>{name} has swept the step and folded the blankets. “Slept well? Good.”<br>{name} pours the tea. “No rush this morning. Sit a while.”
Bold{name} hauls in an armful of firewood. “Breakfast after. Chores first, together.”<br>{name} plants a hand on the table. “Big plans today. You’re in, right?”<br>{name} grins over her mug. “I was up an hour ago. Took you long enough.”
Gritty{name} is mending a chair by the hearth. “Almost done. Then we eat.”<br>{name} shakes the frost off her boots. “Cold one. Kettle’s on for you.”<br>{name} nods at the door. “Rain later. We’ll beat it if we start now.”
Clever{name} looks up from her notes. “I’ve planned the whole day. Want to hear it?”<br>{name} slides the almanac over. “Look: perfect weather for the east rows.”<br>{name} taps the window. “The swallows are back early. Good sign.”
  • "Rose Cottage misses you" names the house with residents slept in least lately once it's been 8 nights or more (a house you've never slept in doesn't), with Tonight › (it becomes tonight's house) and Another night (the line put away for the day). Never a penalty.

Household milestones

The married count opens the household's milestones, each news once in the morning as the count passes it ("Married 5 · Household scene 1, the furniture set, +5% items"). The crew's +5%s and the second evening read the count live. The Hearts header always says "Married n/N".

MarriedOpens
1The homestead (House 1 to build); the night’s companion and her house; the household’s meals; anniversaries
5Household scene 1; the furniture set (five pieces); +5% items crew-wide
10Household scene 2; two evenings every fourth day; two invitations a morning
15Household scene 3; +5% more items crew-wide
20The finale, the Great Harvest; then the Festival of Vows each year

The furniture set comes at 5 married: five pieces, placed one a house from the homestead's sheet, as words until decorations draw them:

  1. Patchwork Armchair
  2. Pine Bookcase
  3. Copper Kettle Stand
  4. Window-Seat Cushions
  5. Braided Hearth Rug

Household scenes, the finale and the Festival of Vows

  • Household scenes (at 5, 10 and 15 married) and the finale (20) are Ready offers, never auto-played. From the morning after the wedding that reaches them, the morning's Hearts lines ("⌂ Household scene 1 · Ready", with Play) and the Hearts header show them, and they play over any page: the opening, each spouse's beat in wedding order, then the close, the pack's where written and the game's short card where not (a line, her one line, a line). Every spouse wears the scene's own outfit, else the dinner dress (the finale: the kimono). A choice in them is wording only. They pay nothing, are read once, and the Diary's Memories shelf replays them. Every scene is offered, written or not.
  • The finale, the Great Harvest, is Ready the morning after the wedding that makes 20 married, once: its opening at the festival ground, its dance (a play whose grade pays nothing; without the pack's, "Dance with everyone under the lanterns."), each spouse's beat, its close, and then the game's last stage-up card, "The Great Harvest", with every spouse's face. The game goes on.
  • The Festival of Vows falls each later year on the finale's day: every spouse +50 that morning (a Hearts line, "♥ The Festival of Vows · 20 spouses · ♥ +1000 · festival night tonight") and a festival night that evening, in its own slot.

Bonds and ties

Each pair of Umas on the farm holds bond points, whole, that never fall. They grow when two share a place, five bond hearts come at fixed thresholds, the authored ties seed them, and each new heart opens the pair's conversation. Farmhands who work the same place the same day build theirs at night; the ties' seeds and shared houses build the rest.

 Stand-in 2 joins Stand-in 1 ──► their tie seeds: Stand-in 1 & 2 at 150 (♥♥); Stand-in 3 joins: 1 & 3 at 50 (♥)
   │                                the next morning: "♥♥ Stand-in 1 & Stand-in 2 · bond 2 · Read"
   ▼
 two Umas at the forest all day ──► that night: +10 a pair (+15 if both favour foraging; ×1.5 more at Romance 4)
   │                                 290 → 300: ♥♥♥, the morning lists "bond 3"
   ▼
 the next night, at the forest again ──► +10% of each one's night's items, copies into the Warehouse
                                          the morning: "Side by side · +1 item"

What a pair earns:

SharedPointsPaid by
Working the same place the same day (the Fields’ tenders together)+10 a pair; ×1.5 when both favour that place’s linebondsTonight, night step 6
An expedition party, a day away+15 a pairaddBond, from expeditions (not built yet)
A festival team+20 a pairaddBond, from the festivals (not built yet)

The same house, a night+10 a pairshareTheHouses, night step 6
Romance 4: her bond with her closest friend×1.5 on any of these, once (never ×2.25 when both qualify)bondFactor, in addBond
  • The same place the same day: every pair among the Umas who worked one place today earns +10, once a night (a running job, or the Fields' tenders with a growing block, who count as one place). ×1.5 when both have the same favourite line and it's that place's (two foragers at the forest or the Sawmill, two farmers tending). An Uma works one place a day, so a pair earns this once.
  • What pays today: the same place, the same breakfast and the same house. An expedition party, a festival team and a staff meal pay once those features exist.
  • Romance 4: what a pair earns is ×1.5 when either Uma is at Romance 4 or later and the other is her closest friend; once, never ×2.25 when both are.
  • Whole: each pay rounds once, half up, after its factors: two foragers who are closest friends, one at Romance 4, earn 10 × 1.5 × 1.5 = 22.5, so 23.

The ties' seeds. When she joins, each of her ties whose other Uma is on the farm starts at its seed (a seed never lowers a bond). An Uma whose tie you haven't met has no bond with her yet.

  • A seeded heart is a new heart: its conversation opens and the next morning lists it, so an arriving Uma comes with what her closest ties already share.
  • Her closest friend is her first tie by one order, over all her ties, on the farm or not: the strongest, then the most bond points, then the ties' order. Her profile's Ties use the same order. Her closest friend's loved gift shows from Friendship 7, Romance 4 grows their bond, her wedding room defaults to her friend's house, and her two closest friends on the farm are her wedding guests.

Side by side

At 3 bond hearts two Umas work side by side:

WhereBeside a 3-heart partnerHow
A work place (gathering, a station)+10% of her night’s itemsNight step 4 (nightExtras): copies of the items her work finished tonight, the same goods and stars, share × n whole and one more by draw(uid, day, 'bond:<id>', 0), each picked by draw(…, k); into the Warehouse. Her partner worked the same place today
  • Why at night: a day's haul is planned when its job starts, so a bonus at planning would miss a partner who starts later. Side by side pays as a night extra, after the hauls, reading the bonds as they stand then: a pair who reach 3 hearts tonight work side by side from the next night. The job's own plan never changes.
  • In her element (Friendship 8) makes it ×1.2 for her at her line's place; the perks' own pot follows it (see the perks at her line, above).
  • The Fields: her partner's block must have a growing plot. The block's card says "· side by side", and its nights left count it.
  • The morning says "Side by side · +3 items", the night's extras in all, opening the Warehouse.

Pair conversations

  • Each new heart opens one conversation, a tie's pair's at that level: the pack's pair file where it writes the level, else the game's card, two short lines, one each. The stand-in's first two Umas have all five written; her farmer and fisher, and her forager and fisher, play the cards.
  • A pair with no tie has no conversation: the heart's line is "‹a› and ‹b› are closer now", with no scene.
  • Read plays the pair's opened and unread levels in turn in the scene player ("Stand-in 1 & Stand-in 2 · Bond 1 (1/2) · Fields"), each line a tap, the speaker's bust (both in the level's own outfit, else the day's season; her face the line's emote). A choice is wording only. The end, or Skip, marks every level read, once. It pays nothing. A replay from the Diary marks nothing new.
  • The morning lists the bond hearts reached since the last morning, one line a pair at its highest heart, in the cast's order: "♥♥ Stand-in 1 & Stand-in 2 · bond 2" with Read while a conversation up to that heart waits (a seed from 0 to 2 plays bonds 1 and 2), the heart alone once read, or the generic words for a pair with no tie.
  • The Diary's Conversations lists every pair with a heart (the most hearts first), its conversations read as Bond n replays and ♥♥ Read for the unread.

The romance at night

Every step of the night, the romance's among them, is in the calendar and the day. An authored arrival joins with her ties seeded, and the night clears the whole of the day's romance record.

The morning's Hearts section comes after Ranks. Each line is a tap to her profile (or where it says), in this order:

OrderLines
0The night's companion, once married: "☾ Morning with Stand-in 1", with Play (her greeting again; it played as the morning came up)
1Ready, read live: up to three in the People page's order ("● Ready · Stand-in 1 · Friendship 1 · the Fields", with Play), then "+2 more" (People filtered on Ready); then the household's Ready offers ("⌂ Household scene 1 · Ready", with Play)
2This morning's invitations, with Yes, tonight and Another day
3The house's morning ("⌂ Farmhouse · Stand-in 1 · ♥ +30 · ☕ 3 fed · +1 item") with its house line under it; a spouse who moved into a room ("⌂ Stand-in 2 moved into Rose Cottage", with Play: her moving in); "‹house› misses you" with Tonight › and Another night; morning items ("♥ Stand-in 2 left you ★3 Shiitake"); the night's chores
4Birthdays and anniversaries, today's before tomorrow's, birthdays first ("★ Stand-in 1's birthday today", "♥ Stand-in 1's anniversary · ★5 Carrot left for you"); the Festival of Vows
5Levels reached overnight ("♥ Stand-in 1 · Spouse 2, reached overnight", New, playing her scene, then her card)
6The perks' extras ("Perks · +2 items"), then side by side ("Side by side · +3 items"), then each pair's bond hearts

Within a place the night's order holds, each Uma in the cast's order. The section shows whenever a stored line or a Ready Uma is there. There's no pop-up per event: the morning, her ● Ready chip and ♥ on her pip wherever the map draws her are the romance's only signals, and pips stay their places' taps. The household milestones are news in the morning's section after it.

Scene links

The players open over any page from a query param, so the game's buttons are links. A link plays only what the game has given you: a typed, bookmarked or stale one (yesterday's morning line, a meal from another day, someone you haven't met) closes quietly as it opens, with no scene, no pay and no picture in her gallery. Each kind has one pure rule in romance/links.ts, read once as its player opens; the game's own buttons only ever open what these allow, so their flows are unchanged:

LinkPlaysOnly when
?life=morning:<id>The night’s companion greets youThe morning after a night she was your companion (love.night is yesterday): that morning only
?life=moving-in:<id>Moving inA spouse with a room, on her wedding day or the morning she moved into a room she waited for
?life=invited-to-work:<id>The invitation to workThe day you invited her (roster[id].farmhand is today)
?life=breakfast:<id>, ?life=dinner:<id>A meal’s lineThat meal cooked today, and she’s a spouse who ate it (or the one whose line it plays)
?event=<id>Her heart eventReady (readiness); with no action left it says so and plays nothing
?ask=<id>Ask herShe said “Not yet” at her confession, and you haven’t asked today
?memory=<id>:<rung>A Memory, paying nothingA Memory already played: her meeting scene, or a rung of her route
?evening=<id>Her kinds sheet, then the eveningHer evening is open to her now: in front of you (home on the farm, a farmhand there by day, or out in town today with you there), a hangout or a date her ladder opened, a slot free
?evening=pickTonight’s pickerSomeone can spend an evening now, and the day has its 2 actions
?occasion=<id>An occasion sceneToday’s occasion, with an Uma you’ve met, not yet played with her today
?household=<id>A household scene or the finaleReady (the night saw its count) and not yet read; with &replay=1, once read
?level=<id>:<s2|s4>A spouse level’s scene and cardShe’s reached it (it pays nothing)
  • Read once, as it opens: a scene that has started plays to its end (the evening's play, scene and card; a meal's line) whatever the save says after.
  • The morning's ☾ line plays her greeting again that morning; the next morning it's gone with the night.
  • Pays nothing either way: the life scenes, Memories, a night level's and a household replay never pay; a heart event, Ask her, an evening and an occasion pay only through their own rules (playEvent, askHer, spendEvening, playOccasion), which refuse the same things.

Part 4 — The save

The save, as a player meets it

One save, on the phone first

  • One save per player: the signed-in Google account's farm, one copy on the phone and one on the server.
  • Every change is saved on the phone at once. Play never waits on the network once the phone holds a save.
  • A sync follows 1.5 seconds after the last change, when the app comes back to the foreground, when the network returns, and as the app opens.
  • Opening the app: splash, then Google sign-in, then the farm. The app opens the phone's save for this account; with none, the server's; with none there either, a new farm on day 1. A save from before v5 stops at the start-over card first (see Save versions).
  • The app waits for the story pack as it opens; if the pack can't load, the app stops and offers Try again.
  • A new phone needs the server once. Offline with no save on the phone, the app stops with "The game couldn't open on this phone." and Try again, since a new farm made offline could later outrank the real one.
  • Only a player's own change moves the save's "last changed" time. Starting the new farm over an older save and syncing never do.

What a sync does

A sync reads the server's save, then does one of three things:

The phoneThe serverWhat happens
No changes since the last syncNewer than the phone'sThe phone takes the server's save
No changes since the last syncThe sameNothing
Changes waitingUnchanged since the phone's last sync, or emptyThe phone uploads; the server stores it as the next revision
Changes waitingChanged by another deviceThe conflict rule (below)
  • Someone else wrote first: if the server moved on between the read and the upload, the app checks again against what's stored now and retries, up to three times in one sync. Changes still unsent after that wait for the next sync, and Account says "Sync paused".
  • A change made during a sync starts another sync right after.

The conflict rule. When the phone and the server both changed, the save changed last wins whole; a tie keeps this phone's. If the server's wins, this phone's unsynced save is kept first as a safety copy ("Replaced by a newer save"), and every page says "A newer save replaced this device's changes. They're kept in Account." until Account is opened. If the copy can't be kept, nothing is replaced and the sync pauses.

A refused upload. The server stores what the app sends and never edits a save. It refuses: - a save it can't read (a bad shape, unknown keys, or a version other than v5: an old build's older save); - a save belonging to another account; - a save of an older version than the one it holds.

Each refused upload is kept on the server as a rescue copy (the newest one), so play that couldn't be uploaded isn't lost; nothing restores it automatically. The phone keeps its changes, and Account says "Sync paused".

What the app says

StateAccount's "Save" rowThe bar's sync chip
SyncingSyncing…—
OfflineOffline · saved on this phoneOffline
A sync failed, changes waitingSync paused · saved on this phoneSync paused
A sync failed, nothing waitingSync pausedSync paused
Changes waiting for the next syncSaved on this phone—
The server holds everythingSynced—
Never syncedNot synced yet—
  • Account also shows Last synced, the build, master data's release and the story's (Story: "Release 4, 14 Nov 2026", or "No story yet"). Account scrolls, its panels two to a row where they fit. The chip opens Account.
  • "Not backed up since …" shows on every page once a sync has failed (not merely offline) with changes waiting and the last good sync is ten minutes old or more: "Not backed up since 8 Nov, 14:05. Your progress is only on this device." It opens Account.

Safety copies

Account lists the newest five safety copies this phone keeps for the account, each with why and when:

ReasonWhen it's kept
Replaced by a newer saveA sync replaced unsynced changes on this phone
Before a restoreA restore replaced the save that was playing
Couldn't be readThe phone's save couldn't be read (it's kept as it was, and the app opens the server's)
Before the new farmThe farm started over from an older save that held unsynced play
  • Restore puts a copy back as the newest change, so it uploads over the server's. The save it replaces becomes a "Before a restore" copy first, so a restore can be undone.
  • A copy this build can't play does nothing on Restore. A copy older than v5 shows Kept instead of Restore, since restoring it would only start a new farm again.

The update prompt

The app keeps itself on the phone. A new build downloads in the background and waits: a card at the top says Update ready, "Restart to use the new build.", with a Restart button. Nothing reloads until Restart is tapped. The card sits outside the opening screens, so it shows even when the app stopped while opening.

Save versions

VersionWhat it isWhat a player meets
v5The farm: the one save, its field a grid of tiles and its day a budget of actionsEvery new farm starts on it, and the app plays and writes it. It's kept: later changes grow it in place or migrate it, never start it over
v4The farm of blocks and Umas' jobs, before the day of actionsThe start-over card, then a new farm on v5
v1, v2, v3Older formats, from before the farm started over the first timeThe start-over card, then a new farm on v5
Newer than v5—Refused: this build doesn't read it
  • Why a new version, not a v4 change. The field's blocks became tiles, the work board's jobs and the stations' multi-day jobs became the day's actions and a station's one load, and today counts the actions: a played v4 can't be read as a tile farm. A new version lets the rules that already guard an older save do the work: the start-over card, the safety copy, the server's backup, and an older build refusing a v5 rather than misreading it.
  • v5 is kept from now on. The owner plays the deployed farm as the real game, so no change starts it over: a new field is grown in place (an older save reads it as its default, and updatedAt never moves for it), and a change a default can't cover migrates the save as it loads. The start-over card stays only for a save from before v5.
  • The start-over card. When the phone's save, or on a new phone the server's, is older than v5, the opening stops at one card: "Your farm starts over", "This version of the game begins a new farm on day 1.", and Start the new farm. Nothing changes until the button is tapped.
  • The new farm is day 1 on v5: 500g, 9 Carrot seeds, the field overgrown but for its cleared patch of nine, no station loads, ten actions, the Dorm at level 1, the Farmhouse, nobody on the roster and the farmer's name empty. Only the account carries over, its "last changed" time with it, so the farmer's name is the new farm's first change. Then the opening runs as on any new farm.
  • v5 reads what v4 grew into: a save with no diary.notes loads with no Town notes unlocked, a morning's "New story arrived" line is only on a morning after a new release, her chapter is read from her route, and the save takes festival night on any day (once at most) and a festival's news by any festival's id, since the story says which days hold one.
  • v5 grows in place with upkeep and loans: farm.loans, farm.owed, farm.short and farm.income are new; a save without them reads as no loans, nothing owed and no nights, and the morning's upkeep section (summary.money) is absent on a morning from before them. Nothing migrates, and updatedAt never moves for it.
  • v5 grows in place with food: farm.pantry (the pantry's meals, by the day cooked and their stars) is new; a save without it loads with an empty pantry, so a live farm's first night with food buys its takeout. The morning's food lines (pantry, takeout) are two more kinds in summary.money, a farmhand's board may hold Cook ({ kind: 'cook' }) and her practice Cooking (cook). Nothing migrates, and updatedAt never moves for it.
  • v5 grows in place with the calendar: today.occasions (who played today's occasion scene) is written only once one plays, and a save without it has played none; the morning's New Year's and story days' lines are two more kinds of news. Nothing migrates, and updatedAt never moves for it.
  • v5 grows in place with the Uma loop: each field is written only once it's used, and a save without it reads as before: roster[id].farmhand (the day you invited her to work; none, no farmhand), today.meals (the household's meals cooked today; none, none), homestead.companion (tonight's pick; none, the night's default), love[id].night (the night she was last your companion; none, never) and the morning's "☾ Morning with" line (morning); and an evening may be Dinner in town (dinner) or A night in (night-in). Nothing migrates, and updatedAt never moves for it.
  • v5 grows in place with the illustrations: gallery (her illustrations seen, by her id: the scenes' ids in the order first seen) is new; a save without it has seen none. Nothing migrates, and updatedAt never moves for it.
  • v5 grows in place with the farmhands: roster[id].tasks (her board; none, no tasks), roster[id].practice (her actions by aptitude; none, none) and today.fed (the farmhands well fed this morning; none, nobody) are written only once used; the morning's farmhand (farmhand) and aptitude (aptitude) lines are new kinds; and a station's load may hold up to 20 (a farmhand's bigger load at her yield). Nothing migrates, and updatedAt never moves for it.
  • v5 grows in place with the field's plots: fields.plots (each plot's 40 tiles, plot 1 first) holds the field; a save from before plots holds one field's tiles (fields.tiles) and reads as plot 1, exactly as it was, tile for tile, crops, growth and watering untouched; a farmhand's field task with no plot is plot 1's. The save is written with fields.plots from the player's next change, and nothing moves updatedAt for it.
  • Nothing goes silently: a phone save with unsynced play is kept first as a "Before the new farm" safety copy (if it can't be kept, nothing changes and the card says so, with the button to try again). The new farm syncs up as it is, and the first time the server stores a v5 over the older save it copies the older one, whole, to a backup (saveBackups/{uid}-v4 for a v4). The server never writes into a save.
  • An older save met later (the server's in a sync, or in a conflict) becomes the same new farm and uploads.

A newer save on an older build

A save newer than v5 comes only from a newer build. When this one meets it: - As it opens (the phone's save, or on a new phone the server's): the opening screen says "This save is from a newer version of the farm." with Try again, and the app asks for the new build at once, so the update prompt comes up; Restart brings a build that can play it. - On the server, at a sync: the sync stops before any upload ("Sync paused"), so nothing writes over the newer save. - An upload of an older version the server receives anyway is refused and kept as a rescue copy.

A save the app can't read at all on the phone is kept as a "Couldn't be read" safety copy, and the app opens the server's save instead.
